  - Trim the whitespace right of (h) — either extend panel (h) to the full figure width or move the colorbar to a horizontal strip, recovering pixels for the data panels.
  - Confirm RGB is preserved on export; do not pre-convert to CMYK.

## Caption & Legend Auditor

### efigure1.png

---
TITLE:              Missing — caption opens with "Kernels at the same design resolution…"; no bold lead-in title sentence.
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) linear scale near the main lobe, (b) dB relative to peak; both described in order and panel labels are lowercase as required.
ERROR BARS:         Not present — figure shows deterministic/analytic smoothing kernels, so no error bars are expected; caption should say so explicitly.
SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated in part — series length N = 256 given; but the number of realizations/windows is not stated (needed only if any curve was estimated rather than computed analytically — currently ambiguous).
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is involved (acceptable), and equivalent degrees of freedom ν are given for every kernel (satisfies the journal's DOF requirement). Absent: definition of ν (equivalent/effective degrees of freedom of the spectral estimator, and how it was computed), and the frequency/offset at which the quoted "40 dB" skirt difference is measured.
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: 𝒦(f) (smoothing/spectral window kernel), N, Δ (sampling interval), R (design resolution bandwidth), L (number of tapers), ν, "dB relative to peak" reference level, and the ordinate normalization 𝒦(f)/NΔ. Also undefined: the multitaper time–bandwidth product (NW) implied by L = 7.
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legend in panel (a) plus redundant color naming in the caption; note the caption must state that the same colors/line styles apply to panel (b), which has no legend.
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the caption defers to "Table 2" and to "recipe (b′)", which is defined only in the main text; the meaning of "then box" / "then parabola" (post-smoothing of the periodogram with a boxcar or parabolic kernel of matched half-power width) is never explained; the ordinate quantity 𝒦(f)/NΔ is never defined.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold descriptive title as the first sentence of the legend.
  - [BLOCKING] Core symbols are undefined in the legend: 𝒦(f), N, Δ, R, L, ν. A reader cannot interpret either axis without the main text.
  - [BLOCKING] "recipe (b′)" is an unresolvable external cross-reference; it also collides notationally with panel label (b), creating genuine ambiguity about whether (b) refers to a panel or a recipe.
  - [BLOCKING] Ambiguity about whether curves are analytic kernels or Monte-Carlo estimates. If any curve is estimated, the number of realizations/windows must be stated per the journal's statistical-reporting rule; if all are analytic, this must be said.
  - [ADVISORY] "the skirts differ by 40 dB" is unquantified — no frequency offset, no pair of curves identified.
  - [ADVISORY] Multitaper kernel is specified only by L = 7; the time–bandwidth product NW (and taper family, e.g., Slepian/DPSS) is needed to reproduce the curve.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) has no legend; caption does not state that the panel (a) key applies to both panels.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) curves are clipped at −100 dB (Bohman trace runs off the axis); caption should note the axis truncation so the reader does not read the clip as a feature.
  - [ADVISORY] Panels (a) and (b) use different abscissa ranges (±10 vs ±25 in fNΔ); this should be stated to prevent misreading the two panels as the same window.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption does not state whether "half-power width" matching was done numerically or analytically, yet the sentence "All main lobes have nearly the same half-power width" is the figure's main claim — quantify the residual spread (e.g., ±x%).
  - [ADVISORY] Check final-size type: at 181 mm double-column width the axis-label and legend text should be verified at ≥6 pt (target 8–10 pt); the two-line legend entries are the smallest text present. Supply as TIFF/EPS/PDF at ≥600 DPI for final submission (current PNG is review-grade only).
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a bold title, e.g.: "**Smoothing kernels matched at a common design resolution trade main-lobe shape against sidelobe suppression.**"
  - Insert definitions on first use: "𝒦(f) is the spectral smoothing kernel of the estimator, normalized by NΔ (N = 256 samples, Δ = sampling interval); R = 8/(NΔ) is the design (half-power) resolution bandwidth; L is the number of tapers; ν is the equivalent degrees of freedom of the resulting spectral estimate (computed as [state formula])."
  - Replace "recipe (b′)" with a self-contained phrase, e.g., "…followed by parabolic smoothing of matched half-power width (the cosine-taper-plus-parabola design)"; reserve (a)/(b) exclusively for panel labels.
  - State explicitly: "All kernels are computed analytically; no data realizations are involved, so no error bars are shown."
  - Quantify the closing claim: "At |f|NΔ = 20, sidelobe levels span ≈40 dB, from −33 dB (untapered, then box) to −78 dB (Bohman, then box)."
  - Specify the multitaper design: "Slepian (DPSS) tapers with time–bandwidth product NW = 4, L = 7 tapers."
  - Add the key-transfer and axis notes: "Line colors and styles are identical in both panels; the legend in (a) applies to (b). Note the wider abscissa range and the −100 dB display floor in (b)."
  - Add the matched-width tolerance: "Half-power widths agree to within x% of R across all five kernels."

### efigure2.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — "Dropping the leakiest taper." is present as an opening phrase but is not set in bold, and it is written as a conclusion rather than a neutral descriptive title.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete (a and b described in order), but panel (b) is under-specified — it does not say **which** curves appear in (b) (the grey periodogram appears to be absent, yet the text refers to "the smoothed periodogram" tracking the truth), nor whether the dashed L=6 trace is plotted there.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars or shaded intervals are drawn. However, the variance/noise content is the whole point of the figure, so the equivalent statistical descriptor (degrees of freedom) must be complete; see STATISTICAL DETAIL.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — "One realization," N = 1024 samples. Advisory: state the random-seed/realization provenance and whether this realization is representative (i.e., that conclusions hold across realizations), since all claims in (b) rest on a single draw.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — absent items:
                      • Equivalent degrees of freedom (ν) for the **adaptively weighted L = 7** estimate (all other five traces have ν quoted). Adaptive weighting yields a frequency-dependent ν; give its range or median, or state explicitly that ν varies with f.
                      • The Slepian time–bandwidth product NW (or W) used to generate the L = 6 and L = 7 taper families — L alone does not define the estimator.
                      • Definition of ν as "equivalent degrees of freedom" (symbol used six times, never expanded).
                      • The smoothing-window bandwidth convention for the box (half-power width? total width?) — only the parabola's convention is given.
                      • The "25 % cosine taper" parameterization (25 % of the record total, or per end).
                      • No hypothesis test is performed; this is acceptable for this figure type, but the quantitative claims ("floors 20 dB high", "halves that") should be reported as measured numbers with the frequency band over which they were evaluated.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density), AR(4) (fourth-order autoregressive), ν (equivalent degrees of freedom), L (number of tapers), R (smoothing bandwidth / half-power width), Δ (sampling interval), N (record length in samples), f (frequency), NW (not present but required).

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present in panel (a) as an in-figure legend, and duplicated in the caption. BLOCKING gap: panel (b) has no legend and the caption never states that the panel-(a) line styles/colors carry over to (b).

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot reproduce or fully interpret the estimators without NW, the smoother bandwidth conventions, the adaptive-weight ν, and an explicit key for panel (b). The AR(4) coefficients (or a pointer to where they are specified) are also needed to know what "True PSD" is.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees of freedom are quoted for five of six estimates but omitted for the adaptively weighted L = 7 estimate — the journal requires the estimator's degrees of freedom wherever noise levels are compared, and panel (b) is explicitly a noise-floor comparison.
  - [BLOCKING] Panel (b) has no color/line-style key and the caption does not state that the panel-(a) legend applies to (b); the grey periodogram appears in the (a) legend but not in (b), so the legend cannot be assumed transferable.
  - [BLOCKING] Slepian time–bandwidth product (NW) for the multitaper estimates is never given; "Multitaper, L = 7" is not a complete estimator specification.
  - [BLOCKING] Title sentence is not bold and is interpretive rather than descriptive.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption states the untapered box "floors 20 dB high" and L = 7 "floors 10 dB high", but panel (b) shows the green trace asymptoting near 18 dB and the blue band centered near 9–13 dB. Quote measured values with the averaging band, or soften to "≈".
  - [ADVISORY] The caption's final clause credits "the smoothed periodogram" with tracking the truth, but the only smoothed-periodogram trace in (b) is the orange cosine-taper-plus-parabola curve; the wording conflicts with the legend label and with the grey "Periodogram" entry.
  - [ADVISORY] The dashed L = 6 trace is essentially invisible in (b) (obscured by the solid L = 7 band), so the caption's claim that "dropping the seventh taper halves that" cannot be verified from the figure as drawn.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption labels do not match in-figure legend text verbatim ("untapered periodogram smoothed with the box" vs. "Untapered, then box"; "cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed with a parabola" vs. "25 % cosine taper, then parabola").
  - [ADVISORY] Single realization with no indication of reproducibility (seed, or statement that behavior is typical).
  - [ADVISORY] At 2078 px across a 181 mm double-column width the effective resolution is ≈291 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum for color line art. Supply vector EPS/PDF or re-render at ≥2140 px (≥600 DPI preferred for line art).
  - [ADVISORY] AR(4) process parameters/coefficients are not given or cross-referenced, so "True PSD" is not independently interpretable.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Recast the caption with a bold, descriptive title and a complete key, e.g.:
    "**Effect of dropping the leakiest Slepian taper on multitaper spectral estimates of an AR(4) process.** (a) One realization (random seed 0) of a fourth-order autoregressive (AR(4)) process, N = 1024 samples, sampling interval Δ, smoothing/half-power bandwidth R = 8/(NΔ). Traces: periodogram (grey; ν = 2 equivalent degrees of freedom, EDOF); untapered periodogram smoothed with a boxcar of half-power width R (green; ν = 17.2); equal-weight multitaper estimates using Slepian tapers with time–bandwidth product NW = 4, L = 7 (solid blue; ν = 14) and L = 6 (dashed blue; ν = 12); adaptively weighted multitaper estimate, NW = 4, L = 7 (dotted blue; ν varies with frequency, median ν = X, range X–X); periodogram of the 25 %-cosine-tapered record smoothed with a parabolic window of half-power width R (orange; ν = 16.7); and the true power spectral density (PSD, black). (b) Each estimate minus the true PSD; line colors and styles as in (a), and the grey periodogram is omitted for clarity. Over 0.35 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.5 the mean bias is +XX dB (untapered box), +XX dB (L = 7), +XX dB (L = 6), +XX dB (adaptive), and +XX dB (cosine-taper/parabola). No hypothesis tests were performed; a single realization is shown and is representative of M = XX independent realizations."
  - Add the adaptive-weight ν (median and range, or a note that it is frequency-dependent).
  - State NW explicitly, and give the bandwidth convention for both smoothers (boxcar and parabola).
  - Either add a small legend to panel (b) or state in the caption that (b) uses the (a) key and that the periodogram is omitted.
  - Replace the rounded "20 dB / 10 dB / halves" claims with measured band-averaged bias values and the band limits.
  - Increase the visibility of the L = 6 trace in (b) (plot it above the L = 7 band, thin the L = 7 line, or use partial transparency) so the central claim is visible.
  - Harmonize caption wording with the in-figure legend labels, and clarify which curve "the smoothed periodogram" refers to.
  - Cross-reference the AR(4) coefficients (equation or table number) in the caption.
  - Supply the final figure as EPS/PDF or ≥600 DPI TIFF at 181 mm width, RGB, and confirm all in-figure text is ≥6 pt (axis/legend text currently scales to roughly 8–9 pt at 181 mm, which is acceptable).

### efigure3.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — first sentence is descriptive but not set as a bold lead-in title ("**Variance, leakage and side-lobe level at matched resolution.**")
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b), (c) described in order and in lowercase, matching the in-figure panel labels
ERROR BARS:         Not present — correctly and explicitly justified ("exact kernel and degrees-of-freedom computations, with no sampling variability")
SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated in the only sense applicable (N = 256 samples); no realizations/windows are involved, and the caption says so
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no inferential test is used or needed, but the estimator's degrees of freedom shown in (a) are not defined (how ν is computed: exact kernel-based ν = 2/Var-ratio vs. Thomson's 2L approximation, and for Welch the overlap-corrected form); the reference level for the dB axis in (c) is also unstated
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: α (time–bandwidth product of the Slepian/multitaper design), Δ (sampling interval), N (stated numerically as 256 but not identified as record length in samples), L (implicitly "number of tapers" — make explicit), "box"/"parabola"/"Gaussian" (smoothing kernels applied to the periodogram), "Bohman" and "25 % cosine taper" (taper definitions), dB reference
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legend with distinct colour + marker + line-style triples; caption points to its location
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — relies on Table 2 for the meaning of the dotted line, and the axis normalisation "half-power width × NΔ" and the dB reference in (c) are not explained in the caption

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] The degrees-of-freedom estimator in panel (a) is not specified. The journal requires stating the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared; since ν *is* the plotted quantity, its definition (exact quadratic-form/kernel computation, Welch overlap correction, Slepian ν ≠ 2L) must appear in the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Panel (c) ordinate is in dB with no stated reference. "Peak beyond 1.5× the half-power half-width (dB)" is uninterpretable without "relative to the kernel peak at zero lag/frequency (0 dB)".
  - [ADVISORY] No bold lead-in title sentence; the legend opens with a sentence fragment lacking a verb.
  - [ADVISORY] Caption defers to "Table 2" for the meaning of the dotted vertical line; a reader of the figure alone cannot interpret it. State the value inline (e.g., "the reference resolution 8/(NΔ) used throughout").
  - [ADVISORY] Axis normalisation "half-power width × NΔ" is never defined; state that width is the full width at half maximum of the smoothing kernel in frequency, normalised by the Rayleigh/frequency resolution 1/(NΔ), so the abscissa is dimensionless.
  - [ADVISORY] Symbols α, Δ, L and N are used in the legend/caption without definition.
  - [ADVISORY] The two multitaper Slepian curves differ only by line style and the multitaper family shares one colour; at 88 mm reduction the dashed vs. dotted vs. solid blue distinction plus open-circle/open-square/triangle markers may be hard to resolve. Caption should at least name the encoding (colour = method family, line style = taper count) so the key is unambiguous.
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2086 px across a 181 mm double-column figure is ≈293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum and well below the 600 DPI preferred for coloured line art. Supply vector PDF/EPS for production.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b)/(c) ranges are cut off for several methods (curves terminate at different abscissa values, e.g. Hann-then-Gaussian ends near 10, Welch extends to 24.5). The caption should explain why sweeps have different extents (parameter admissibility / integer taper or segment counts), otherwise the truncation looks like missing data.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) uses a log ordinate spanning ~7 decades with no statement of whether the quantity is normalised to unit total kernel power; state the normalisation.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the opening as a bold title, e.g.: "**Variance, spectral leakage and side-lobe level of smoothed-periodogram and multitaper estimators compared at matched resolution.**"
  - Add a definitions sentence: "N = 256 samples, Δ = sampling interval, α = time–bandwidth product, L = number of tapers. Abscissae give the kernel full width at half maximum normalised by 1/(NΔ) (dimensionless)."
  - Define the ν estimator: "In (a), ν is the exact equivalent number of degrees of freedom obtained from the second moment of the estimator's quadratic form (Welch curves include the overlap correction); it is not the 2L approximation. All curves are deterministic kernel computations, so no error bars or sample sizes apply."
  - In (c), add "ordinate is in dB relative to the kernel peak (0 dB at zero frequency offset)".
  - Replace the Table 2 cross-reference: "The dotted vertical line marks the reference half-power width of 8/(NΔ) used for the comparison in Table 2."
  - State normalisation in (b): "Kernel power is normalised so that the total kernel integrates to unity."
  - Add one sentence on the encoding and sweep extents: "Colour and marker identify the method (in-figure legend, lower right); solid/dashed/dotted blue distinguish the three multitaper variants. Sweeps terminate where the bandwidth parameter reaches its admissible limit, so curves cover different width ranges."
  - Supply the figure as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI raster) and verify all tick and legend text is ≥6 pt at the final double-column width.

### efigure4.png

---
TITLE:              Missing — caption opens with "Fig.~5 in full.", a cross-reference rather than a bold, standalone descriptive title.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Partial — rows (a–d), (e–h), (i–l), (m–p) are described, but the four *columns* are delegated to Fig. 5 ("Columns and colours as in Fig.~5"). No panel is individually described; panel (c) in particular differs from its row-mates (ordinate is normalized, $h_{k-n}/\max h$) and panel (b) plots a modulated taper $h_k\cos(2\pi f'k\Delta)$ with a four-level $f'N\Delta$ key — neither is mentioned.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — figure shows deterministic/analytic curves and weight ladders. This is acceptable, but the caption should say explicitly that all quantities are computed analytically (no Monte-Carlo realizations), otherwise a reader cannot tell whether the curves are estimates.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — the design parameters that play the role of n are never given in the caption: series length $N$ (=256 from the abscissae), sampling interval $\Delta$, analysis bandwidth $R$, segment length/overlap for the Welch column, smoothing half-width for column 2, sinc cutoff for column 3, and $L=7$ appears only inside the figure. For column 1 the number of segments (7, from panel (i)) is not stated.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — the equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu$ (13.4, 16.7, 17.2, 14.0) are printed in panels (i)–(l) but the symbol $\nu$ is never defined in the caption, nor is the estimator/definition used to compute it (e.g. $\nu = 2(\sum c_i)^2/\sum c_i^2$). No hypothesis test is performed, so test statistic/p-values are not applicable — state this explicitly ("no statistical tests are performed; all curves are analytic") so the omission reads as intentional. Half-power widths and out-of-band leakage percentages are quoted without saying how they were computed (integration limits, frequency grid) or that they are exact rather than estimated.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: $\nu$ (equivalent degrees of freedom), $Q$ (the quadratic/kernel matrix whose eigenvectors are shown), $c_i$ (eigenweights), $\mathcal{K}(f)$ (spectral concentration kernel), $R$ (analysis bandwidth), $N$, $\Delta$, $L$, $i$, $k$, $n$, $f'$, $h_k$ / $h_k^{(i)}$ / $h_{k-n}$, "Slepian sequences", "Thomson's estimate", dB reference convention. "eigen-taper" and "eigen-weight ladder" are non-standard coinages and should be defined on first use.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Missing in caption — colour-to-method mapping (pink = Welch, orange = periodogram smoothing, green = sliding sinc, blue = multitaper) is carried only by the in-figure column titles and by a cross-reference to Fig. 5. The lightness ramp encoding $i=1\ldots4$ appears as an in-figure legend in (d),(e) only and is not restated; the meaning of the grey dotted vertical lines is given ($|f|=R/2$) but $R$ itself is undefined. The light-grey horizontal gridlines in (m)–(p) are unexplained.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the caption cannot be read without Fig. 5 (columns, colours, methods), and the key parameters ($N$, $\Delta$, $R$, segment count, $L$) plus the definitions of $\nu$, $Q$, $c_i$ and $\mathcal{K}(f)$ are absent.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold, self-contained figure title as the first sentence of the legend.
  - [BLOCKING] Caption is a cross-reference stub ("Fig.~5 in full", "Columns and colours as in Fig.~5"). Column identity, method names and colour coding must be restated in this legend.
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees of freedom $\nu$ are displayed in (i)–(l) and noise/variance performance is implicitly compared across the four estimators, but $\nu$ is neither defined nor its estimator given — directly violates the journal requirement to state the estimator's degrees of freedom where noise levels are compared.
  - [BLOCKING] No statement of the number of realizations/windows behind the displayed quantities, or that the figure is analytic rather than simulated. As drafted a reader cannot tell.
  - [BLOCKING] Sample-size-equivalent design parameters missing: $N$, $\Delta$, $R$, number of Welch segments and their overlap, smoothing kernel width, sinc cutoff, and $L$.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined symbols/abbreviations in the legend: $Q$, $c_i$, $\mathcal{K}(f)$, $R$, $\nu$, $f'$, $h_k^{(i)}$, $h_{k-n}$, dB reference.
  - [ADVISORY] The four column titles ("Average segments (Welch)", "Smooth one periodogram", "Slide a sinc window", "Multitaper, $L=7$") are titles set inside the figure; IEEE TBME asks that titles be moved to the caption. Convert them to short in-axes labels or fold them into the legend text.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) uses a different ordinate normalization ($h_{k-n}/\max h$) from (a),(b),(d); this asymmetry is invisible in the caption and looks like an error.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) shows only three visually distinguishable orange traces for four legend entries ($f'N\Delta=0..3$); the $f'N\Delta=0$ trace overlaps the flat plateau. Caption should note the overlap, or the linestyles should be differentiated.
  - [ADVISORY] Only four eigen-tapers are shown per method although the ladders run to 7–12 non-negligible weights; caption says "first four" but should justify the truncation and note that the (i)–(l) abscissa extends to $i=12$ with zero-height bars.
  - [ADVISORY] Numerical claim "5.84, 8.03, 8.03 and $7.41/(N\Delta)$" applies the unit only to the last value; rewrite so the $1/(N\Delta)$ unit clearly governs all four numbers, and state the definition of "half-power width" (−3 dB full width).
  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2088 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI minimum and well below the 600 DPI preferred for coloured line art. Re-export as vector (PDF/EPS) or at ≥600 DPI.
  - [ADVISORY] Confirm axis/tick label font is ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at 181 mm final width; the $i$, $k$ and $fN\Delta$ labels look small in a 16-panel grid.
  - [ADVISORY] Lightness-ramp encoding of $i$ within a single hue may be hard to separate in print and for low-vision readers; consider adding linestyle variation.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the legend as a standalone unit, e.g.: "**Taper banks, eigen-structure, weight ladders and spectral kernels for four spectral estimators.** Columns, left to right: (i) Welch averaging of overlapping segments (pink); (ii) smoothing of a single periodogram (orange); (iii) a sliding sinc window (green); (iv) Thomson's multitaper estimate with $L=7$ Slepian tapers (blue). Rows: (a)–(d) the taper bank as constructed; ... (e)–(h) the first four eigenvectors ("eigen-tapers") of the quadratic form $Q$ ...; (i)–(l) normalized eigenweights $c_i/\sum_i c_i$ ...; (m)–(p) the estimator kernel $\mathcal{K}(f)$ in dB relative to its peak, with dotted lines at $|f|=R/2$."
  - Insert all design parameters: "$N=256$ samples, sampling interval $\Delta$, analysis bandwidth $R=\,$___$/(N\Delta)$; Welch: ___ segments of ___ samples with ___% overlap; periodogram smoothing half-width ___; sinc cutoff ___; multitaper $L=7$, $NW=$___."
  - Define the degrees of freedom explicitly: "Equivalent degrees of freedom $\nu=2(\sum_i c_i)^2/\sum_i c_i^2$, shown in the upper right of (i)–(l): $\nu=13.4$, 16.7, 17.2 and 14.0 respectively."
  - Add an explicit statement in lieu of error bars and n: "All curves and numbers are computed analytically from the estimator definitions; no simulated realizations are involved, hence no error bars are shown, and no statistical tests are performed."
  - State the colour/lightness key in the caption: "Within each panel of (a)–(h), increasing lightness denotes taper index $i=1$ (darkest) to $i=4$ (lightest); the same hue identifies the method throughout."
  - Note the panel-(c) normalization and the panel-(b) modulation explicitly, including the meaning of $f'$ and why $h_{k-n}/\max h$ is plotted.
  - Move the four in-figure column titles into the caption (or reduce them to italic axis-group labels) to comply with the no-in-figure-titles rule.
  - Restate the leakage metric precisely: "Fractional power outside $|f|\le R$, computed by numerical integration of $\mathcal{K}(f)$ on a ___-point grid over the Nyquist band: 0.05%, 0.13%, 1.39% and 0.22% for columns 1–4."
  - Define the horizontal gridlines in (m)–(p) or remove them.
  - Re-export as vector PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, <10 MB, sans-serif fonts at 8–10 pt final size.

### figure1.png

---
TITLE:              Missing — the legend opens with a descriptive clause ("Three routes to one estimate of…") but there is no short bold title sentence as required.
PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b), (c) described in order and matching the lowercase in-figure labels.
ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows a single deterministic realization (one 8-s epoch); no error bars or distributions are plotted. This is currently implicit and should be stated.
SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — N = 1024 samples, one epoch, one channel, 128 Hz. (Number of subjects/records = 1 should be made explicit rather than "the authors' archive".)
STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no inferential test is performed, so test statistic/p-values are not applicable; however (i) the number of realizations/windows behind the sliding-sinc average is not given (it is N − N_s + 1 or similar and should be stated numerically), and (ii) the equivalent degrees of freedom ν = 17 is given but its definition is off-loaded to a manuscript section, and no ν is given for the box-smoothed periodogram or the sliding-window estimator, even though the three estimators' noise levels are implicitly compared.
ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (expand at first use, "electroencephalogram"); Δ (sampling interval, used in R = 8/(NΔ)); α = 4 (time–bandwidth/half-bandwidth parameter — symbol never named); λ_i (called "eigenvalues" but not identified as the Slepian concentration eigenvalues of the specified NW); ν (named "equivalent degrees of freedom" but the estimator it belongs to and how it was computed are only in the text); Ŝ(f) (y-axis symbol not mentioned in caption); "max." in panel (c) axis.
COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legend in (a) plus redundant color/linestyle naming in the caption; (b) curves annotated in-figure and (c) guide line identified as dotted. Note that the pink sliding-sinc trace in (b)/(c) and the pink dotted trace in (a) are the same color family but different line styles — worth one clarifying phrase.
SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the cross-reference to Section~\ref{sec:periodogram} for the definition of ν, the undefined α and Δ, the unspecified number of sliding windows, and the vague data provenance ("the authors' archive", no ethics/consent statement) prevent stand-alone reading.
ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold title sentence at the head of the legend; IEEE TBME style requires a brief descriptive caption with no in-figure title, and the reviewer checklist requires the title as the first (bold) sentence.
  - [BLOCKING] Number of realizations/windows behind the averaged sliding-sinc estimate in (a)–(c) is not stated; the journal explicitly requires the number of realizations/windows behind any averaged quantity or distribution.
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees of freedom are reported only for the multitaper estimate and only by cross-reference; since (b) compares agreement/noise floors across three estimators, ν (or the equivalent smoothing bandwidth) must be stated in-caption for each estimator.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined symbols/abbreviations in the caption: EEG, Δ, α, and the identity of Ŝ(f); a reader cannot reconstruct R = 8/(NΔ) = 1 Hz or the taper design from the caption alone.
  - [ADVISORY] Data provenance is vague ("de-identified scalp EEG … from the authors' archive"); IEEE TBME expects an IRB/ethics-approval or "de-identified, exempt" statement, and the caption should state that n = 1 record/subject is shown as an illustrative example, not a population result.
  - [ADVISORY] Absence of error bars is not explained; state explicitly that a single epoch is shown so no variability estimate is plotted.
  - [ADVISORY] Numerical agreement values ("3×10⁻¹³ dB", "0.06 dB") are given without specifying that these are maxima over 0–40 Hz (panel (b) shows a range spanning ~10⁻¹⁵–10⁻¹³ dB); clarify whether the quoted numbers are maximum or median absolute differences.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) x-axis is N_s/N (1–16) while the caption speaks of "window length N_s"; align the wording with the plotted abscissa and state the fitted or theoretical slope basis for the 1/N_s guide.
  - [ADVISORY] The 1/N_s guide line is a visual reference, not a fit; say so to avoid it being read as a regression.
  - [ADVISORY] Color usage: the pink dotted trace in (a) and the pink solid trace in (b)/(c) should be explicitly tied together; also confirm the green/blue/pink set remains distinguishable in grayscale and for color-vision deficiency, since (a) relies on three overlaid curves.
  - [ADVISORY] Check axis/tick label point size at final double-column width (181 mm) — the (b) and (c) tick labels are the smallest text and must remain ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred). Also supply the final file as TIFF/EPS/PDF at ≥600 DPI; the 1830-px PNG is review-grade only.
SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the legend opening with a bold title, e.g.: "**Three equivalent routes to the same 1-Hz-resolution PSD estimate.**"
  - Insert definitions on first use: "electroencephalogram (EEG)"; "sampling interval Δ = 1/128 s"; "time–bandwidth product α = NWΔ = 4"; "Ŝ(f), estimated one-sided power spectral density"; "λ_i, the concentration eigenvalues of the i-th Slepian taper (i = 1,…,N)".
  - Add the realization count: "the sliding-sinc estimate is the average of M = N_s-length windows slid one sample at a time (M = … for N_s = 16N)".
  - Report degrees of freedom for all three estimators in-caption and remove the section cross-reference, e.g.: "Equivalent degrees of freedom: ν = 17 for the multitaper estimate, ν ≈ … for the box-smoothed periodogram and ν ≈ … for the sliding-sinc average (computed as 2/Var-ratio of the log-spectrum)."

Also missing: how "Noise" (standard deviation) and "Bias" were obtained (analytic prediction vs. Monte-Carlo estimate), and how "Total" is formed from them (e.g. √(bias² + variance)).

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Estimator degrees of freedom are displayed in the figure (ν = 2, 17, 98) but nowhere defined in the caption, while the figure explicitly compares noise levels. Journal policy requires the estimator's degrees of freedom to be stated where noise levels are compared.
  - [BLOCKING] Abscissa quantity RNΔ is undefined: R, N and Δ are not all defined in the caption, so the x-axis is uninterpretable without the main text.
  - [BLOCKING] The caption is not self-contained: the circled points are identified only by cross-reference to another figure. State what each circled resolution is (ν and RNΔ value) in this caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Title not bold; convert the first sentence to a bold run-in title.
  - [ADVISORY] Raster resolution is marginal: 990 px wide corresponds to ~283 DPI at 88 mm single-column width and only ~138 DPI at 181 mm double-column width. Supply vector EPS/PDF (preferred for line art) or re-render at ≥600 DPI at the intended final width.

  - [ADVISORY] Number of frequency bins entering each RMS average (whole band vs. peak region) is not given, so the effective averaging behind each plotted point is unknown.
  - [ADVISORY] Marker shapes (filled circle = whole band, open square = peak region) are not described in the caption; if the figure is reproduced in grayscale or reduced, the in-figure labels may become the only cue.
  - [ADVISORY] Units/normalization of the amplitude axis x_k in panel (a) are not stated (arbitrary units?), and the taper is described as "scaled to the record's peak amplitude" without stating that this scaling is for display only.

  - Recast the caption along these lines: "**Choosing the resolution.** (a) The record of Fig. X: N = 1024 samples of the AR(4) (fourth-order autoregressive) process (black, arbitrary units), the 25 % cosine taper scaled to the record's peak amplitude for display (dashed), and the tapered record (orange); k is the sample index. (b) Root-mean-square (RMS) error, in dB on the log power-spectral-density (PSD) scale, of the cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed by a parabolic kernel of half-power width R, relative to the true PSD, plotted against the dimensionless resolution bandwidth RNΔ (Δ = sampling interval); each point is the average over n = 300 independent realizations and over M = ... frequency bins (whole band) or M = ... bins in the peak region 0.09 ≤ fΔ ≤ 0.16. Curves are ensemble averages; no error bars are shown because the realization-to-realization spread is smaller than the symbol size (max s.d. = ... dB). The smoothed estimator has ν ≈ ... equivalent degrees of freedom (ν = 2 at RNΔ = 0, ν = 17 at RNΔ = 8, ν = 98 at RNΔ = 48). Gray curves: analytic noise (estimator standard deviation, falling with R) and bias (kernel blur, growing with R). Filled circles, solid black: total error over the whole band; open squares, dashed: total error over the peak region. Circled points mark the three resolutions shown in panels (b)–(d) of Fig. X: too noisy (ν = 2), just right (ν = 17) and too smooth (ν = 98). Dotted vertical lines mark R equal to the width of a single peak and to the peak separation."

  - [BLOCKING] Caption/axis mismatch, panel (c): caption describes the quantity as Σ_{i≤L} λ_i|H^(i)(f)|²/(2αΔ) but the axis reads 𝒦(f)/NΔ. Either define 𝒦(f) ≡ Σ_{i≤L} λ_i|H^(i)(f)|² (with the stated normalization) in the caption or relabel the axis; also reconcile the 2α vs N divisor.

State explicitly that all curves are exact deterministic evaluations of the Slepian sequences and their spectra (no Monte-Carlo realizations, no averaging, no error bars), and give the equivalent degrees of freedom ν ≈ 2L implied by each L in (c).

  - [ADVISORY] Dotted vertical lines at fNΔ = ±4 in (b) are explained ("|f| ≤ R/2"), but the identical dashed vertical lines in (c) at ±4 (the "Box" edges) are not distinguished from them; clarify.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (d): the abscissa extends to i = 17 but the number of tapers evaluated is not stated; also state that values below ~1e−16 are at double-precision floor, since 1−λ_1 ≈ 1e−10 and the axis runs to 1e−16.

  - [ADVISORY] Resolution: 2092 px across a 181 mm double-column width gives ≈293 DPI, marginally under the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art (600 DPI preferred). Re-export as vector EPS/PDF, which also removes the concern entirely.

### figure5.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — the opening phrase ("Three ways to build a bank of tapers from one window, and the reference bank") reads as a title but is not typeset bold and is a fragment rather than a sentence.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a)–(d) and (e)–(h) are each described in order; the (a–d)/(e–h) grouping sentences are helpful.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — deterministic/analytic curves and weight ladders; no variability is plotted. The caption should say so explicitly so a reader does not wonder whether spread is omitted.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated (partially) — record length N=256, seven Welch segments, L=7 Slepians, "four shown" in (b)–(d) are given. Missing: the total number of eigen-weights plotted in (e)–(h) (the abscissa runs past 10 in (f), (g) with no stated upper limit), and the number of sinc-window positions actually existing in (c) versus the four drawn.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — the estimator degrees of freedom ν is reported for all four routes (13.4, 16.7, 17.2, 14.0) and its defining formula is given, which satisfies the journal's degrees-of-freedom requirement. Absent: (i) statement that no hypothesis test is performed and no realizations/windows underlie these curves (they are analytic), (ii) the bandwidth–duration product (NW or time–bandwidth) corresponding to R and L=7, which is needed to reproduce the Slepian bank, (iii) the number of data windows/realizations that would be implied by ν if the panels were compared to Monte-Carlo noise levels.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: Δ (sampling interval, appears in R=8/(NΔ), in the (b) legend f′NΔ, and in the (b) ordinate); f′ (frequency offset/shift variable of the (b) legend and ordinate); k (sample/time index on all abscissae of (a)–(d)); n (shift index in h_{k−n}); i (taper/eigen-weight index); λ_i (Slepian eigenvalue, invoked in (c)); c_i (called "eigen-weight" but never defined as an object — only its normalized ratio is plotted); "max h" (normalization in the (c) ordinate); L (stated only inside the figure title of (d), not in the caption text). "Welch", "Hann", "Slepian", "Thomson" are proper names and acceptable; "half-power width" should be tied to R explicitly.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial — the caption states one colour per route with lighter shades for later bank members, and (b) and (d) carry in-figure legends. Panel (c) has four curves with no in-figure legend and no caption mapping of shade to window position/sample offset; panel (a) has seven overlapping curves with no indication of which is which (the caption's "lighter shades" rule is not visibly applied in (a), where all seven appear the same colour). The colour correspondence between the top row and the matching bar colour in the bottom row should be stated explicitly.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — (1) Δ, f′, λ_i, n, and max h are undefined; (2) the caption cites "\eqref{eq:ladder}" and "Fig. S4", so the reader must leave the figure to see the equation's provenance and the eigen-tapers/kernels; (3) the abscissa of (e)–(h) is not explained (i indexes eigen-weights in decreasing order — say so), nor is the reason (f) and (g) extend past i=10 while (e) and (h) stop at 7.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] No bold title sentence; the legend opens with a sentence fragment. IEEE TBME expects a brief descriptive caption whose first sentence identifies the figure content, and this journal's house style for this submission requires it bold.
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined symbols that carry the quantitative content: Δ, f′, λ_i, n, c_i, and "max h". Without Δ the bandwidth R=8/(NΔ) is dimensionally meaningless to the reader, and without f′ the (b) legend entries cannot be interpreted.
  - [BLOCKING] Degrees-of-freedom context incomplete for a noise-level comparison: ν is quoted to three significant figures for four estimators but the caption never states that these are analytic (zero-realization) values rather than estimates from data, nor gives the time–bandwidth product that fixes the L=7 Slepian bank. A reader cannot reproduce ν=14.0 from the caption alone.
  - [BLOCKING] In-figure panel titles ("Average segments (Welch)", "Smooth one periodogram", "Slide a sinc window", "Multitaper, L=7") violate the journal instruction that figures carry no titles; this text must move into the caption or be recast as short in-axes annotations.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (c) has four unlabelled curves and no legend; the shade-to-position mapping is unstated. Panel (a)'s seven curves are all one shade, contradicting the caption's "lighter shades for later members in (b–d)" only by implication — state that (a) uses a single shade deliberately.
  - [ADVISORY] The upper limit of i in (e)–(h) is not stated, and the caption does not say that weights are sorted in descending order or that near-zero bars were retained rather than truncated.
  - [ADVISORY] External dependencies (\eqref{eq:ladder}, Fig. S4) reduce self-containment; the ν formula is fortunately reproduced inline, so the equation cross-reference can simply be dropped or made parenthetical.
  - [ADVISORY] Raster resolution: 2088 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈293 DPI, marginally below the 300 DPI floor for colour line art. Supply vector (PDF/EPS) or re-render at ≥600 DPI for the final version. Also confirm that the smallest tick labels are ≥6 pt at 181 mm.
  - [ADVISORY] "four of a continuum" in (b) is elegant but ambiguous; state how the four f′NΔ values were chosen (integer multiples of 1/(NΔ)).

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Add a bold title sentence, e.g. "**Three routes from a single window to a bank of tapers, and the resulting eigen-weight ladders.**"
  - Define every symbol on first use in the caption: "Δ, sampling interval; N=256 samples; R=8/(NΔ), half-power bandwidth; k, sample index; n, shift in samples; f′, frequency offset; i, taper index in order of decreasing weight; c_i, eigen-weight of taper i; λ_i, Slepian concentration eigenvalue; h_k, window sequence; L, number of tapers."
  - State the Slepian design parameters explicitly: "Slepian tapers with time–bandwidth product NW = ... , L = 7, equal weights c_i = 1/7."
  - Add a variability/realization sentence: "All curves and weights are analytic; no data realizations are involved, hence no error bars. ν is the analytic equivalent degrees of freedom from (…), not an estimate, and is reported for a single record of N=256 samples."
  - Explain the bottom row's abscissa and extent: "(e–h) Normalized eigen-weights c_i/Σc_i in decreasing order; ladders of length 7, 12, 11 and 7 respectively, with bars below 10⁻³ visible as near-zero."
  - Give the colour/shade key completely: "Colours identify the route (pink, Welch; gold, smoothed periodogram; green, sinc window; blue, multitaper) and are shared between the top and bottom rows; within (b–d) lighter shades denote later bank members, while the seven copies in (a) are drawn in a single shade. In (c) the four shades correspond to window offsets n = … samples."
  - Move the four in-figure panel titles into the caption text and delete them from the image.
  - Replace "\eqref{eq:ladder}" with the inline formula only, and reduce the supplement pointer to a parenthetical ("eigen-tapers and smoothing kernels in Fig. S4").
  - Re-export as PDF/EPS (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, ≤10 MB, and verify minimum font size ≥6 pt at final width.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — the figure shows single-realization spectral estimates and deterministic kernels; no error bars, shaded bands, or distributions appear, so no error-bar definition is required. However, see STATISTICAL DETAIL for the 300-realization summary numbers quoted in the text of the caption.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Stated — record length N = 1024, "one realization" for the displayed curves, and "300 realizations" for the ensemble median absolute errors. Segment parameters for (d) (nine segments, 192 samples, step 104) are also given. Good.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — absent items:
  - No dispersion or interval for the ensemble median absolute errors (5.3, 2.3, 1.0, 1.0 dB over 300 realizations); a median quoted without an IQR or bootstrap CI cannot be compared by the reader.
  - The claim "This realization has more leakage than is typical" is a comparative statement with no supporting quantity (e.g., the percentile of this realization within the 300-realization distribution).
  - The band over which "median absolute errors" are computed is not specified (whole Nyquist band? the roll-off region above the peaks?).
  - No statistical test, test statistic, or p-value is reported for the implicit comparison among the four estimators. If the paper claims one estimator is better, a paired test across the 300 realizations with exact p-value is expected; if no inferential claim is intended, say so explicitly ("no inferential test performed; values are descriptive").
  - Positively: the estimator degrees of freedom ν are given for all four methods (17.2, 14.0, 16.7, 17.6), satisfying the journal requirement that DOF be stated where noise levels are compared. ν itself is never defined in the legend.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: PSD (power spectral density); ν (equivalent degrees of freedom of the estimator); R (smoothing half-bandwidth / analysis half-width, used in "R = 8/(NΔ)" and "|f| ≤ R" and "1.5R"); Δ (sampling interval); N (record length, inferable but not stated); L (number of tapers — used but not named); 𝒦(f) (kernel, the y-axis of e–h, never defined in the legend); "Recipe (b′)" (an internal label from the main text with no meaning here); "Fejér tails" and "Slepian taper" (acceptable domain terms, but Fejér kernel should be identified as the squared Dirichlet/rectangular-window kernel on first use in a caption).

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial — the in-figure key appears only in panel (a) ("True PSD" in black, "Estimate" in green), and the legend states "true PSD black". Panels (b), (c), (d) use blue, orange and pink estimates with no key in-panel or in-legend. A reader scanning panel (d) alone cannot confirm that pink is the estimate and black is truth. Add one sentence stating that in all top-row panels the black curve is the true PSD and the colored curve is the estimate, colors distinguishing methods only and carrying no quantitative meaning.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the legend depends on three external pointers a reader cannot resolve from the figure: "the record of Fig. \ref{fig:motif}", "the exact estimate \eqref{eq:identity}", and "Recipe (b′)"/"Table \ref{tab:ensemble}". The generating process for the true PSD (a two-peak spectrum with a steep roll-off) is never described, so the magnitude of the leakage floor cannot be interpreted independently. The inset axes in (e–h) are also unexplained (zoom of the kernel main lobe around f Δ = 0.4, gray box showing the zoomed region).

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Legend has no bold lead-in title. IEEE TBME requires no title inside the artwork and a brief descriptive caption; the house style used for this manuscript sets the first sentence in bold. Currently the first sentence is a cross-reference-laden fragment.
  - [BLOCKING] "Recipe (b′)" is an undefined internal label that also appears as in-figure annotation text in panel (c). Either spell out the method in the panel annotation and legend ("cosine taper + parabolic smoother") or define the label in the legend.
  - [BLOCKING] PSD is used in the in-figure key and in the legend without expansion; expand at first use in the caption.
  - [BLOCKING] Ensemble statistics (300-realization medians) are quoted with no dispersion measure and no definition of the frequency band over which the error is taken, so the comparison among the four estimators is not verifiable from the figure and legend alone.
  - [ADVISORY] ν, R, Δ, L and 𝒦(f) are used as bare symbols; each should be named once in the legend, since 𝒦(f) and ν are the two quantities the whole figure turns on.
  - [ADVISORY] Colour-to-method mapping is given only implicitly, via the in-panel key in (a). Panels (b)–(d) have no key.
  - [ADVISORY] Insets in (e–h) are undescribed: state that they magnify the kernel main lobe over 0.39 ≤ f Δ ≤ 0.41 and that the gray rectangle marks the magnified region.
  - [ADVISORY] The in-plot annotations ("No taper, then box", "Multitaper, L = 7", "Recipe (b′)", "Welch, 9 segments") sit where a reviewer may read them as panel titles, which TBME disallows. They are acceptable as data labels, but consider moving them into the legend or reformatting them (e.g., lower-case, adjacent to the curve) to avoid an editorial query.
  - [ADVISORY] The claim "This realization has more leakage than is typical" invites the question of why an atypical realization was plotted. Justify briefly, or plot a median-error realization and report the atypical one in the text.
  - [ADVISORY] The four half-power widths are listed as "8.2, 7.2, 8.2 and 7.8/(N Δ)"; the shared unit attached only to the last value is easy to misread. Write "8.2, 7.2, 8.2 and 7.8, all in units of 1/(N Δ)".
  - [ADVISORY] Check that no text in the rendered figure falls below 6 pt at the final column width. At 2077 px across a 181 mm double-column figure the raster is ~290 DPI, marginally under the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art; TBME prefers 600 DPI for line art. Re-export as vector PDF/EPS, which also removes the DPI question entirely.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel (b) shows the estimate stopping short of f Δ = 0.5 while the other panels reach the Nyquist edge; if that is a plotting artifact, fix it, and if it is intentional (taper-dependent valid band) state it in the legend.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Rewrite the legend along these lines:

    "**Four practical spectral estimators and their kernels.** All estimates are computed from the single N = 1024-sample realization of Fig. \ref{fig:motif}, whose true power spectral density (PSD) is the black curve in (a)–(d); the coloured curve in each of (a)–(d) is the estimate, colour distinguishing method only. Smoothing half-width R = 8/(N Δ), where Δ is the sampling interval; ν is the equivalent degrees of freedom of each estimator. (a) Untapered periodogram smoothed with a box, the exact estimate of \eqref{eq:identity} (ν = 17.2). (b) Thomson's multitaper estimate with L = 7 Slepian tapers (ν = 14.0). (c) Cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed with a parabola of matched half-power width (ν = 16.7). (d) Welch's method, nine Hann segments of 192 samples stepped by 104, chosen for matched half-power width (ν = 17.6). Half-power widths are 8.2, 7.2, 8.2 and 7.8, all in units of 1/(N Δ). Median absolute error over 0.2 ≤ f Δ ≤ 0.5 for this realization: 11.6, 4.6, 0.9 and 0.8 dB; over an ensemble of n = 300 independent realizations: 5.3 (IQR x–y), 2.3 (x–y), 1.0 (x–y) and 1.0 (x–y) dB (Table \ref{tab:ensemble}). This realization was chosen to show more leakage than is typical; it lies at the pth percentile of the ensemble for method (a). Values are descriptive; no inferential test was performed. (e)–(h) Corresponding smoothing kernels 𝒦(f) for (a)–(d), plotted as in Fig. \ref{fig:motif}, with 1.4 %, 0.22 %, 0.12 % and 0.48 % of kernel power outside |f| ≤ R. Insets magnify the main lobe over 0.39 ≤ f Δ ≤ 0.41 (gray rectangle marks the magnified region). The untapered box (e) inherits the Fejér (squared-Dirichlet) tails, and its estimate levels off ≈20 dB above the true PSD. The seventh Slepian taper places the multitaper skirt at −22 dB (f), and that estimate levels off ≈10 dB above truth. The cosine-taper/parabola kernel (g) has no side lobes and falls below −38 dB beyond 1.5R. Welch's kernel (h) has no flat top and side lobes spaced at the scale of the spectral peaks."
  - Add the true-PSD/estimate key to all four top-row panels, or state the mapping once in the legend as above and delete the in-panel key from (a) for consistency.
  - Specify the frequency band used for the median absolute error, and add IQR or 95 % bootstrap CI for each of the four ensemble medians.
  - Define ν, R, Δ, L, 𝒦(f) and PSD on first use in the legend; replace "Recipe (b′)" with the descriptive method name in both the panel annotation and the legend.
  - Re-export as vector PDF or EPS (RGB, sans-serif, ≥6 pt at 181 mm width) to satisfy the ≥300/600 DPI line-art requirement; confirm file size <10 MB.
  - Verify panel (b)'s estimate extends to the same frequency limit as the other panels, or explain the truncation in the legend.

### figure7.png

---
TITLE:              Present but **not bold** — "The estimators used in practice agree to within their own noise." is an appropriate one-sentence title but is not typeset in bold as the first sentence of the legend.

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a) and (b) are described in order with lowercase labels matching the figure.

ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars, shaded bands, or distributions are drawn. The caption does, however, compare estimator noise levels and correctly states the equivalent degrees of freedom (ν = 14, 16.7, 17.6) for each estimator, which satisfies the journal's degrees-of-freedom requirement.

SAMPLE SIZE:        Missing — the caption never states (i) that this is a single EEG epoch (n = 1 realization), (ii) the epoch duration and sampling rate, (iii) the number of frequency bins over which the median and 95th-percentile differences in (b) were computed, or (iv) the number of tapers/segments for the *cosine-tapered periodogram* branch. "Nine Hann segments" is given for Welch only.

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no inferential test is claimed (appropriate here), but the descriptive statistics are incompletely specified: no count of frequency bins behind the median/95th percentile, no frequency range over which they were evaluated (0–40 Hz? excluding the 0–1 Hz roll-off, which visibly dominates the negative excursions?), and the noise benchmark "4.3√(2/ν)" is given as a bare formula without stating that it is the asymptotic standard deviation of a log-spectral estimate in dB. No p-values are needed, but the comparison "medians are smaller than the estimators' own noise" is an implicit claim that should be quantified as a ratio or stated as descriptive only.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG; L (number of tapers — inferable from figure legend but not defined in prose); ν (equivalent/effective degrees of freedom); R (half-power width of the smoothing parabola — symbol used without definition); dB reference level for Ŝ(f) (dB re what? µV²/Hz?); "recipe (b′)" is an internal cross-reference that carries no meaning inside the caption.

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present — in-figure legends in both panels, and colors are additionally named in the caption (blue/orange/pink). Note the caption↔figure wording mismatch flagged below.

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — the data epoch is identified only by cross-reference ("the EEG epoch of Fig. X"); "recipe (b′)" and the parabola half-power width R are defined only in the main text; the dB reference and record length/sampling rate are absent.

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Sample size / realization count not stated. Per journal policy the number of realizations or windows behind any noise-level or distributional statement must be given. Here the median and 95th-percentile difference statistics in (b) are summaries over an unstated number of frequency bins, and the number of records/epochs (apparently one) is never declared.
  - [BLOCKING] The noise benchmark is unattributed and unexplained: "4.3√(2/ν)" appears without identifying it as the large-sample standard deviation (in dB) of a log-periodogram estimate, so a reader cannot verify the central claim of the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Title sentence is not bold.
  - [ADVISORY] Terminology mismatch between caption and figure: panel (b) legend reads "Recipe (b′) − multitaper" and "Welch, 9 Hann segments − multitaper", but the caption reports the same quantities as "(smoothed)" and "(averaged)". A reader must guess the mapping.
  - [ADVISORY] Ŝ(f) axis is in dB with no stated reference unit; the ordinate is therefore dimensionally ambiguous.
  - [ADVISORY] The frequency band used for the summary statistics is unspecified. The visibly largest positive excursions (≈12 dB near 9 Hz) and the negative excursions below 1 Hz will strongly affect the 95th percentile; whether the 0–1 Hz roll-off region was included should be stated.
  - [ADVISORY] EEG is not expanded; acceptable only if expanded at first use in the manuscript body.
  - [ADVISORY] Epoch provenance absent: subject/dataset, epoch length, sampling rate, and any pre-filtering. Cross-reference to another figure is not a substitute.
  - [ADVISORY] Reproduction/format: 990 × 1226 px corresponds to ≈84 mm at 300 DPI (single-column, acceptable), but IEEE prefers ≥600 DPI for line art. Supply a vector EPS/PDF for production; PNG is acceptable for review only. Verify all tick/axis labels are ≥6 pt (8–10 pt preferred) at 88 mm final width — the axis numerals appear near the lower bound after downscaling.
  - [ADVISORY] The final sentence attributes the valley-filling to "the side lobes of the seventh Slepian taper" — an interpretive claim with no supporting element in the figure. Either mark an example valley in panel (b) or soften to "consistent with".

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Bold the opening sentence: "**Estimators used in practice agree to within their own noise.**"
  - Add realization/bin counts and record parameters, e.g.: "Single 13.5 s EEG epoch (n = 1 record, sampled at 256 Hz, n = 1080 frequency bins on 0–40 Hz); summary statistics in (b) are over all 1080 bins."
  - Define every symbol in-caption: "L, number of Slepian tapers; ν, equivalent degrees of freedom of the estimator; R, half-power width of the smoothing parabola."
  - Explain the benchmark: "the asymptotic standard deviation of a log-spectral estimate, 10 log₁₀(e)·√(2/ν) ≈ 4.3√(2/ν) dB, equals 1.6, 1.5 and 1.5 dB for ν = 14, 16.7 and 17.6, respectively."
  - Harmonize wording: replace "(smoothed)" and "(averaged)" with "recipe (b′)" and "Welch", matching the in-figure legend verbatim.
  - State the ordinate reference: "Ŝ(f) in dB re 1 µV² Hz⁻¹."
  - Give a one-clause self-contained description of the epoch instead of relying solely on the cross-reference, e.g. "the same eyes-closed occipital EEG epoch as Fig. 1".
  - Specify the band used for the difference statistics and note whether the sub-1 Hz roll-off was excluded.
  - Export as EPS/PDF (or ≥600 DPI TIFF), RGB, <10 MB, and confirm minimum 6 pt type at 88 mm.

### figure8.png

---
TITLE:              Not bold — "Three estimators of a seizure." is present as the first sentence but is not set in bold, and it is uninformatively terse (no mention of EEG, spectrogram comparison, or the equivalent-degrees-of-freedom point being made)

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete but out of order — described as (a), (b), (c,e,g), (d,f), (h); (d) and (f) are introduced after (g)

ERROR BARS:         Not present — no error bars or confidence intervals are drawn. For a figure whose entire argument is estimator variance, this is a substantive gap: the smoothed/multitaper spectra in (d) and (f) invite a ±1 standard-error or χ²-based confidence band, and none is shown or defined

SAMPLE SIZE:        Partially stated / Missing — the number of analysis windows is given (599 windows of 2 s stepped by 1 s), but: (i) the number of subjects/recordings/seizures is never stated (apparently n = 1 patient, 1 channel, 1 seizure — say so explicitly); (ii) the number of pixels entering the histogram in (h) is not stated (599 windows × N frequency bins = ?); (iii) the number of Welch segments per 2-s window in (d,f) is not given (only their 0.7-s Hann length)

STATISTICAL DETAIL: Partial — no hypothesis test is performed, which is acceptable for a descriptive estimator comparison, but the following are absent: (1) the estimator degrees of freedom are given (ν = 2, 6, 8.7, 7.7) — good, and this satisfies the journal's dof requirement — but the *definition* of ν (equivalent degrees of freedom of the χ² approximation) is never stated; (2) no summary statistic for the distribution in (h) (median, IQR, standard deviation, or fraction of pixels within ±2 dB); (3) no statement of how the resolution bandwidths (0.5, 1.7, 2.0 Hz) were defined (half-power? equivalent noise bandwidth?) — the caption says "at half power" only for the parabola; (4) Welch overlap fraction and window count not given, so ν = 7.7 cannot be verified by the reader

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (electroencephalogram/electroencephalography — expand at first use even if standard); PSD (power spectral density, used on every colour bar and y-axis); ν (equivalent degrees of freedom); α (time–bandwidth product NW); L (number of Slepian tapers); "Recipe (b′)" (an internal cross-reference, meaningless without the main text); ELROND (dataset acronym not expanded, no citation or accession/version given); C4 (acceptable as standard 10–20 nomenclature, but naming the montage standard would help)

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Present but incomplete — the in-figure line legend appears only in (f); the caption must state that it also applies to (d). Colour bars are labelled, but the caption should say the colour scale is shared and identical across (c), (e), (g) (it says "on one colour scale of 10 to 45 dB" — good) and that values are clipped at both ends (dark red saturation is visible during the seizure). The grey shading in (a), the black triangles, and the white dashed lines are explained — good

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — "Recipe (b′)" cannot be understood without the main text; ν, α, L, PSD and the smoothing parabola are undefined; the number of subjects and the pixel count behind (h) are missing; the sign convention of the (h) abscissa is only inferable from the axis label

ISSUES:
  - [BLOCKING] Error/uncertainty representation is neither shown nor defined anywhere in the legend. Because the figure's claim is that different estimators trade resolution for variance, the legend must at minimum define the uncertainty implied by ν (e.g., "the 95% interval of a ν-dof χ² estimate is ±X dB") or add confidence bands to (d) and (f).
  - [BLOCKING] Number of realizations behind the distribution in (h) is not stated (journal explicitly requires the number of realizations/windows behind any distribution). "Over all pixels" is not a number.
  - [BLOCKING] Number of subjects/recordings/seizures (apparently n = 1) is never stated; a reader cannot tell whether this is a single exemplar or a pooled result.
  - [BLOCKING] "Recipe (b′)" is an undefined internal label that appears in the in-figure legend, three panel annotations, and the caption; the figure is not interpretable without the main text.
  - [BLOCKING] Resolution: 1835 px across a 181 mm double-column width is ≈257 DPI, below the 300 DPI minimum for colour line art (600 DPI preferred). Re-export at ≥300 DPI, or supply vector EPS/PDF for the line panels.
  - [ADVISORY] Panels (c), (e), (g) carry in-figure title lines ("Periodogram: 2 s by 0.5 Hz, ν = 2.0", etc.). The journal prohibits titles inside figures; these are borderline. Either recast them as neutral annotations (e.g., "ν = 2.0") or move the parameter strings entirely into the caption.
  - [ADVISORY] Panel description order in the legend (a, b, c/e/g, d/f, h) does not follow alphabetical order. Grouping by estimator is defensible, but state the grouping explicitly, e.g., "(c), (e), (g) Spectrograms ...; (d), (f) Spectra ...".
  - [ADVISORY] The line legend in (f) is not stated to apply to (d); a reader may think (d) uses different curves.
  - [ADVISORY] The title sentence does not convey the finding. A caption title should be declarative or at least descriptive of the comparison.
  - [ADVISORY] (d) and (f) use different y-ranges (−10 to 45 dB vs 20 to 80 dB); the caption should note that the ordinates differ so the visual amplitude of the ripple is not directly comparable between panels.
  - [ADVISORY] Colour-scale saturation: ictal power clearly exceeds 45 dB (uniform dark red 300–365 s). State that values are clipped at the scale limits.
  - [ADVISORY] The final sentence ("The channel is the one with the largest ictal rise in 2 to 20 Hz power") describes a selection rule and should be moved next to the channel identification in (a), since it is a selection-bias-relevant detail.
  - [ADVISORY] No de-identification/ethics statement element (IRB or data-use approval / public dataset citation) accompanies the human EEG record.
  - [ADVISORY] Bandwidth definitions are inconsistent: "2 s by 0.5 Hz", "by 1.7 Hz", "by 2.0 Hz" — specify whether these are half-power widths, equivalent noise bandwidths, or 2W for the Slepian family.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Bold the title and make it declarative, e.g.: "**Multitaper and smoothed-periodogram spectrograms of a focal seizure trade frequency resolution for stability.**"
  - Insert the missing n values: "Single de-identified recording from one subject (n = 1 seizure, 1 channel); spectrograms comprise 599 windows; the histogram in (h) pools 599 × 61 = 36 539 time–frequency pixels."
  - Define uncertainty once: "Each estimate is approximately χ²_ν distributed; the corresponding 95% intervals are ±X.X dB (ν = 2), ±X.X dB (ν = 6), ±X.X dB (ν = 8.7) and ±X.X dB (ν = 7.7). No error bars are drawn; ν is annotated on each panel." — this single sentence satisfies both the error-bar and degrees-of-freedom requirements.
  - Expand abbreviations on first use: electroencephalogram (EEG), power spectral density (PSD), equivalent degrees of freedom (ν), time–bandwidth product (α = NW), number of Slepian tapers (L). Expand or cite ELROND with version/accession.
  - Replace "Recipe (b′)" with a self-contained description, e.g., "parabolic (Epanechnikov) smoothing of the periodogram, 2 Hz half-power width (denoted Recipe (b′) in the text)".
  - Add: "The line key in (f) applies to (d) as well."
  - Add: "Colour scale is common to (c), (e) and (g); values outside 10–45 dB are clipped."
  - Add the Welch details needed to reproduce ν = 7.7: segment length 0.7 s, Hann window, X% overlap, K segments per 2-s window.
  - Add a one-line quantitative summary of (h): "median difference X dB, IQR [a, b], Y% of pixels within ±2 dB."
  - Add: "(d) and (f) use different ordinate ranges."
  - State the recording/reference explicitly and the ethics/data-use basis in one clause.
  - Re-export at ≥600 DPI or as vector PDF/EPS at 181 mm width; confirm all in-figure text (including the smallest colour-bar tick labels) is ≥6 pt, ideally 8 pt, at final size, and keep the file under 10 MB.

TITLE:              Present but **not bold** — "Sleep spindles at three resolutions." works as a title sentence but must be set in bold (and note that IEEE TBME wants no title inside the figure; the in-panel method strings are acceptable as annotations, not titles)

PANEL DESCRIPTIONS: Complete — (a), (b–g) with the 1 s / 2 s / 4 s pairing spelled out, and (h) all described in order; left/right (multitaper vs. smoothed) assignment is explicit

ERROR BARS:         Not present — panel (h) shows point markers joined by a line, not dispersion. State this explicitly ("markers are single deterministic design values; no error bars") so a reader does not read the connecting segment as a range.

ABBREVIATIONS:      Undefined: EEG (spell out at first use in caption), N2 (NREM stage 2, AASM criteria), C4-M1 (electrode/reference montage), PSD, dB re 1 µV²/Hz, α (time–bandwidth product NW), L (number of tapers/Slepian sequences), ν (equivalent degrees of freedom), R (frequency-resolution target in panel h — never defined anywhere), and "Recipe (b′)" used in the in-panel annotations of (c), (e), (g) but never tied to the caption's "cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed with a parabola"

COLOR/SYMBOL KEY:   Partial — blue circles / orange squares in (h) are defined (and in-figure text "Multitaper"/"Smoothing" helps); the grey shading in (a) is described as marking spindles but the colour is not named and the scoring source is not given; the orange trace in (a) is identified only by the in-figure label "11–16 Hz, offset" — the offset magnitude and the fact that the orange trace is not to the µV scale are unstated; the colormap identity and displayed range (≈ −6 to +14 dB) are not stated in the caption

SELF-CONTAINED:     No — a reader cannot reproduce or fully interpret the figure without the text: missing sampling rate, filter type/order and zero-phase status, window step (given) vs. FFT length/zero-padding, taper type (Slepian/DPSS), the parabolic smoothing kernel half-width, the definition of R and of ν, the "practical multitaper rule" L = 2α − 1 (rule source), spindle annotation provenance, and subject/recording identification

ISSUES:

  - [BLOCKING] Sample size absent. State n subjects, n recordings, n epochs, and n windows per spectrogram (e.g., "single 40 s N2 epoch from one subject; 391 windows at 0.1 s step").
  - [BLOCKING] Undefined symbol R in panel (h) y-axis labels ("1 s, R = 2 Hz", …). R appears nowhere in the caption; without it panel (h) is uninterpretable.

  - [BLOCKING] Title sentence is not bold.
  - [ADVISORY] ν is reported but its estimator definition and the definition of "achieved half-power width" are not, so the reader cannot check the claim that "twice the box area is approximately ν."
  - [ADVISORY] Abbreviations EEG, PSD, N2, C4-M1, α, L, ν undefined in the caption; the caption should be independent of the main text.

SUGGESTED FIXES:
  - Replace the caption with a self-contained version, e.g.: "**Sleep spindles resolved at three window lengths.** (a) Forty seconds of stage N2 non-rapid-eye-movement sleep (single 40 s epoch, one subject; electroencephalogram (EEG), channel C4-M1, sampled at fs = ___ Hz), zero-phase bandpass filtered 0.3–35 Hz (black) and 11–16 Hz (orange, offset by −95 µV, same µV scale); grey bands mark five expert-scored spindles. (b–g) Time–frequency power spectral density (PSD) estimated from windows of 1 s (b, c), 2 s (d, e) and 4 s (f, g) stepped by 0.1 s (391, 381 and 361 windows, respectively), by the Slepian multitaper estimate with L = max(1, 2α − 1) tapers (left; α = time–bandwidth product) and by a cosine-tapered periodogram smoothed with a parabolic kernel (right; labelled 'Recipe (b′)' in the panels). Both columns share the colour scale at right (turbo colormap, −6 to +14 dB re 1 µV²/Hz; values outside the range are saturated). The white rectangle in each panel spans the window length by the achieved half-power width (half-power width of the effective spectral window), so twice its area approximates the equivalent degrees of freedom ν, printed in each panel annotation. (h) Achieved half-power width and ν for multitaper (blue circles) and smoothing (orange squares) at each setting; rows are labelled by window length and target resolution R. Markers are deterministic design values for a single configuration; connecting segments link the paired methods and are not error bars — no error bars, confidence intervals, or statistical tests are shown."
  - Bold the title sentence and keep lowercase panel labels (a)–(h) as already used.
  - Define R at first use in the caption (target half-power resolution in Hz) or relabel the (h) rows to remove the undefined symbol.
  - Add, in the caption or Methods and cross-referenced, the exact ν definitions (ν = 2L for multitaper; the smoothed-periodogram ν formula) and the parabolic-kernel half-width used at each window length, since the figure's central claim is a degrees-of-freedom comparison.
  - Move the two interpretive sentences to the main text, or recast them as a single neutral clause ("resolution trade-off between time and frequency is visible across rows").
  - Re-export at ≥300 DPI (600 DPI for the line-art panels) as TIFF/EPS/PDF, RGB, ≤10 MB, with all text ≥6 pt (target 8–10 pt) at 181 mm width; confirm final height ≤230 mm.
  - Consider replacing the jet-like colormap with a perceptually uniform one and state the exact colour limits and any saturation in the caption.
